## Combined (all lines)

- Direct path failures: 522 / 1440 (36.2%)
- Gateway failures: 393 / 1440 (27.3%)

### Metric 2: Requests Saved

Gateway-only failures (direct succeeded, gateway failed on the SAME prompt, same instant - the opposite direction): **48**

|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| direct | 918 | 38 | 2989 | 52 | 8763 |
| gateway | 1047 | 48 | 3868 | 66 | 10605 |
